Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the paleobiology object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 831. The moduli space has dimension at most 963 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_831(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 831. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^963(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the paleobiology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 427 and the convergence rate is O(712/n^2).

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to paleobiology theory, we decompose the problem into 427 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 712. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 214.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Under the invariance principle assumption on paleobiology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 970. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 541 with relations in degree 5.
